<div class="bg-base-200 flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center p-6">
    <div class="card shadow-base-300/10 h-full max-h-160 w-full max-w-198 overflow-y-auto shadow-md">

                <h3 class="text-base-content text-xl font-medium">Flyonui Company</h3>
                <p class="text-base-content/80">flyonui@company.com</p>
              </div>

            <!-- Company Info -->
            <div class="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">

                  <div class="bg-warning/20 text-warning rounded-field size-11.5">
                    <span class="icon-[tabler--trending-up] size-6"></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex flex-col">
                  <span class="text-base-content/50 text-sm font-medium">Sales trend</span>
                  <span class="text-base-content text-lg font-semibold">$ 11,548</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="border-base-content/20 rounded-box flex gap-4 border px-4 py-3">
                <div class="avatar avatar-placeholder">
                  <div class="text-success bg-success/20 rounded-field size-11.5">
                    <span class="icon-[tabler--chart-bar] size-6"></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex flex-col">
                  <span class="text-base-content/50 text-sm font-medium">Total Profit</span>
                  <span class="text-base-content text-lg font-semibold">$1735</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="border-base-content/20 rounded-box flex gap-4 border px-4 py-3">
                <div class="avatar avatar-placeholder">
                  <div class="text-primary bg-primary/20 rounded-field size-11.5">
                    <span class="icon-[tabler--discount-2] size-6"></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex flex-col">
                  <span class="text-base-content/50 text-sm font-medium">Discounts</span>
                  <span class="text-base-content text-lg font-semibold">$ 14,987</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="border-base-content/20 rounded-box flex gap-4 border px-4 py-3">
                <div class="avatar avatar-placeholder">
                  <div class="text-accent bg-accent/20 rounded-field size-11.5">
                    <span class="icon-[tabler--wallet] size-6"></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex flex-col">
                  <span class="text-base-content/50 text-sm font-medium">Refunds</span>
                  <span class="text-base-content text-lg font-semibold">$3248</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Revenue Goal Section -->
          <div class="bg-base-200 item-center rounded-box flex justify-center max-md:w-full">
            <div class="bg-base-200 rounded-box space-y-4 p-4">
              <h3 class="text-base-content text-xl font-medium">Revenue Goal</h3>
              <!-- Radial Progress -->
              <div id="revenue-chart" class="w-full"></div>
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-6">
                <span class="text-base-content/80 text-lg">Plan Completed</span>
                <span class="text-base-content text-2xl font-medium">56%</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Bottom Section -->
        <div class="border-base-content/20 rounded-box flex gap-6 border p-6 max-md:flex-col">
          <!-- Sales Plan -->
          <div class="space-y-4">
            <h3 class="card-title">Sales Plan</h3>
            <div class="text-base-content text-7xl font-medium">54%</div>
            <p class="text-base-content/50 text-lg">Percentage profit from total sales</p>
          </div>

          <!-- Cohort Analysis -->
          <div class="space-y-6">
            <h3 class="text-base-content text-xl font-medium">Cohart analysis indicators</h3>
            <p class="text-base-content/50">
              Analyzes the behaviour of a group of users who joined a product/service at the same time, over a certain
              period.
            </p>

            <!-- Statistics Icons -->
            <div class="text-base-content flex gap-6 max-sm:flex-col sm:items-center">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="icon-[tabler--chart-infographic] size-6"></span>
                <span class="text-lg font-medium">Open Statistics</span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="icon-[tabler--percentage] size-6"></span>
                <span class="text-lg font-medium">Percentage Change</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="progress rounded-field h-7 *:rounded-none">
              <div class="progress-bar progress-primary w-full"></div>
              <div class="progress-bar bg-primary/50 w-3/4"></div>
              <div class="progress-bar bg-primary/30 w-2/4"></div>
              <div class="progress-bar bg-primary/10 w-1/4"></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#sale-report-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 246,
            type: "area",
            toolbar: false,
            dropShadow: {
              enabled: true,
              top: 14,
              left: 2,
              blur: 3,
              color: "var(--color-success)",
              opacity: 0.2
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Sales",
              data: [310, 298, 380, 310, 390, 380, 590, 430, 460, 390, 305, 400]
            }
          ],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          stroke: {
            width: 3,
            curve: "smooth"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-success)"],
          tooltip: {
            custom: function (props) {
              // Correctly accessing categories from xaxis
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props

              // Call the buildTooltip function and pass in the relevant options
              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: categories[dataPointIndex],
                hasTextLabel: true,
                labelDivider: ":",
                seriesExtClasses: "gap-2"
              })
            }
          },
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.3,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.3,
              stops: [0, 100]
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: true,
            strokeDashArray: 10,
            borderColor: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
            padding: {
              top: -15,
              bottom: -10,
              left: 0,
              right: 0
            }
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: [
              "January",
              "February",
              "March",
              "April",
              "May",
              "June",
              "July",
              "August",
              "September",
              "October",
              "November",
              "December"
            ],
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            tooltip: {
              enabled: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            crosshairs: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              offsetX: -15,
              style: {
                fontSize: "13px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)"
              },
              formatter: value => (value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}k` : value)
            },
            min: 100,
            max: 700
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/dist/helper-apexcharts.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Online Sales Chart
        buildChart("#online-sales-chart", () => ({
          series: [
            {
              name: "Sales",
              data: [21, 30, 22, 42, 26, 35, 29]
            }
          ],
          chart: {
            height: 200,
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            parentWidthOffset: 0,
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            type: "area"
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            width: 3,
            curve: "smooth"
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          markers: {
            size: 6,
            colors: "transparent",
            strokeColors: "transparent",
            strokeWidth: 4,
            discrete: [
              {
                fillColor: "var(--color-base-100)",
                seriesIndex: 0,
                dataPointIndex: 6,
                strokeColor: "var(--color-primary)",
                strokeWidth: 2,
                size: 6,
                radius: 8
              }
            ],
            offsetX: -1,
            hover: {
              size: 7
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)"],
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.3,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.3,
              stops: [0, 100]
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              show: true,
              style: {
                fontSize: "13px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            min: 10,
            max: 50,
            tickAmount: 4
          }
        }))

        // Daily Sales Bar Chart
        buildChart("#daily-sales-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 150,
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            type: "bar",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            custom: function (props) {
              // Correctly accessing categories from xaxis
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props

              // Call the buildTooltip function and pass in the relevant options
              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: categories[dataPointIndex],
                hasTextLabel: true,
                labelDivider: ":",
                seriesExtClasses: "gap-2"
              })
            }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              barHeight: "100%",
              columnWidth: "12px",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 6,
              colors: {
                backgroundBarColors: ["color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)"],
                backgroundBarRadius: 6
              }
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)"],
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -30,
              left: -16,
              bottom: 0,
              right: -6
            }
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Sales",
              data: [50, 35, 65, 45, 68, 80, 50]
            }
          ],
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              show: true,
              style: {
                fontSize: "13px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/dist/helper-apexcharts.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Performance Chart
        buildChart("#customerRatingsChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 212,
            toolbar: { show: false },
            zoom: { enabled: false },
            type: "line",
            dropShadow: {
              enabled: true,
              enabledOnSeries: [1],
              top: 13,
              left: 4,
              blur: 3,
              color: "#000",
              opacity: 0.09
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Last Month",
              data: [20, 54, 20, 38, 22, 28, 16, 19, 11]
            },
            {
              name: "This Month",
              data: [20, 32, 22, 65, 40, 46, 34, 70, 75]
            }
          ],
          stroke: {
            curve: "smooth",
            dashArray: [8, 0],
            width: [3, 4]
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          colors: ["color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)", "var(--color-primary)"],
          grid: {
            show: false
          },
          tooltip: {
            custom: function (props) {
              // Correctly accessing categories from xaxis
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props

              // Call the buildTooltip function and pass in the relevant options
              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: categories[dataPointIndex],
                valuePrefix: "",
                hasTextLabel: true,
                labelDivider: ":",
                seriesExtClasses: "gap-2"
              })
            }
          },
          markers: {
            size: 6,
            colors: "transparent",
            strokeColors: "transparent",
            strokeWidth: 5,
            hover: {
              size: 6
            },
            discrete: [
              {
                fillColor: "#fff",
                seriesIndex: 1,
                dataPointIndex: 8,
                strokeColor: "var(--color-primary)",
                size: 6
              },
              {
                fillColor: "#fff",
                seriesIndex: 1,
                dataPointIndex: 3,
                strokeColor: "#000",
                size: 6
              }
            ],
            offsetX: -2
          },
          xaxis: {
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 80%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            categories: [" ", "Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            show: false
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

      .apexcharts-flip-y {
        transform: scaleY(-1) translateY(-100%);
        transform-origin: top;
        transform-box: fill-box;
      }
      .apexcharts-flip-x {
        transform: scaleX(-1);
        transform-origin: center;
        transform-box: fill-box;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend {
        display: flex;
        overflow: auto;
        padding: 0 10px;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apexcharts-legend-group-horizontal {
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-group {
        display: flex;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-group-vertical {
        flex-direction: column-reverse;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top {
        flex-wrap: wrap
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-left {
        flex-direction: column;
        bottom: 0;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom.apexcharts-align-left, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top.apexcharts-align-left, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-left {
        justify-content: flex-start;
        align-items: flex-start;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom.apexcharts-align-center, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top.apexcharts-align-center {
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom.apexcharts-align-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top.apexcharts-align-right {
        justify-content: flex-end;
        align-items: flex-end;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-series {
        cursor: pointer;
        line-height: normal;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-text {
        position: relative;
        font-size: 14px;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-text *, .apexcharts-legend-marker * {
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-marker {
        position: relative;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        cursor: pointer;
        margin-right: 1px;
      }

      .apexcharts-legend-series.apexcharts-no-click {
        cursor: auto;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend .apexcharts-hidden-zero-series, .apexcharts-legend .apexcharts-hidden-null-series {
        display: none !important;

      .apexcharts-inactive-legend {
        opacity: 0.45;
      }

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // totalRevenueChart Chart
        buildChart("#totalRevenueChart", () => ({
          series: [
            {
              name: new Date().getFullYear() - 1,
              data: [18, 7, 15, 29, 18, 12, 9]
            },
            {
              name: new Date().getFullYear() - 2,
              data: [-13, -18, -9, -14, -8, -17, -15]
            }
          ],
          chart: {
            height: 300,
            stacked: true,
            type: "bar",
            toolbar: { show: false }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              horizontal: false,
              columnWidth: "30%",
              borderRadius: 8,
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadiusApplication: "around"
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-info)"],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            curve: "smooth",
            width: 4,
            lineCap: "round",
            colors: ["var(--color-base-100)"]
          },
          legend: {
            show: true,
            fontWeight: 500,
            horizontalAlign: "left",
            fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
            position: "top",
            markers: {
              height: 8,
              width: 8,
              radius: 12,
              shape: "circle",
              offsetX: -5
            },
            fontSize: "14px",
            fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
            fontWeight: 400,
            labels: {
              colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 80%, transparent)",
              useSeriesColors: false
            },
            itemMargin: {
              horizontal: 10
            }
          },
          grid: {
            strokeDashArray: 7,
            borderColor: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
            padding: {
              top: 0,
              bottom: -8,
              left: 20,
              right: 20
            }
          },
          fill: {
            opacity: [1, 1]
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul"],
            labels: {
              style: {
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 80%, transparent)"
              }
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              style: {
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)"
              }
            }
          },
          responsive: [
            {
              breakpoint: 1000,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    borderRadius: 9
                  }
                }
              },
              stroke: {
                width: 2.5
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 990,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    borderRadius: 7
                  }
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 900,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    borderRadius: 7
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 2
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 870,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "35%",
                    borderRadius: 7
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 3
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 810,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    borderRadius: 8
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 2
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 768,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "25%",
                    borderRadius: 8
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 4
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 700,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "25%",
                    borderRadius: 7
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 2.5
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 560,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "30%",
                    borderRadius: 7
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 2
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 507,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "30%",
                    borderRadius: 6
                  }
                },
                stroke: {
                  width: 1.5
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 380,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "40%",
                    borderRadius: 5
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ],
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          }
        }))

        // growthChart Chart
        buildChart("#growthChart", () => ({
          series: [78],
          labels: ["Growth"],
          chart: {
            height: 210,
            width: 210,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              size: 150,
              offsetY: 10,
              startAngle: -150,
              endAngle: 150,
              hollow: {
                size: "55%"
              },
              track: {
                background: "var(--color-base-100)",
                strokeWidth: "100%"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  offsetY: 20,
                  color: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 80%, transparent)",
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontWeight: "500",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
                },
                value: {
                  offsetY: -15,
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  fontSize: "24px",
                  fontWeight: "500",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)"],
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shade: "dark",
              shadeIntensity: 0.5,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-primary)"],
              inverseColors: true,
              opacityFrom: 1,
              opacityTo: 0.6,
              stops: [30, 70, 100]
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            dashArray: 5
          },
          grid: {
            padding: {
              top: -35,
              bottom: -10
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/dist/helper-apexcharts.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#shipmentChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            type: "bar",
            height: 300,
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            zoom: {
              enabled: false
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Last Week",
              data: [14, 32, 5, 18, 8, 12, 35]
            },
            {
              name: "This Week",
              data: [33, 38, 10, 28, 27, 30, 40]
            }
          ],
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, #fff)"],
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              horizontal: false,
              columnWidth: "16px",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadiusApplication: "end",
              borderRadius: 6
            }
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Sat", "Sun", "Mon", "Tue", "Wen", "Thu", "Fri"],
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            crosshairs: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontWeight: 400
              },
              formatter: title => title.slice(0, 3)
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              align: "left",
              min: 0,
              max: 40,
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontWeight: 400
              },
              formatter: value => `${value}%`
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "darken"
              }
            }
          },
          responsive: [
            {
              breakpoint: 512,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "12px"
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // totalExportChart Chart
        buildChart("#totalExportChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 250,
            stacked: true,
            type: "bar",
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            y: {
              formatter: function (val) {
                return Math.abs(val)
              }
            }
          },
          legend: { show: false },
          dataLabels: { enabled: false },
          colors: ["var(--color-info)", "var(--color-primary)"],
          grid: {
            borderColor: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
            strokeDashArray: 10,
            xaxis: { lines: { show: true } },
            yaxis: { lines: { show: false } },
            padding: {
              top: -5,
              bottom: -25
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: { filter: { type: "none" } },
            active: { filter: { type: "none" } }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              borderRadius: 5,
              barHeight: "40%",
              horizontal: true,
              endingShape: "flat",
              startingShape: "rounded"
            }
          },
          labels: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul"],
          xaxis: {
            position: "top",
            axisTicks: { show: false },
            axisBorder: { show: false },
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul"],
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "13px",
                fontFamily: "Inter"
              },
              formatter: function (val) {
                return Math.abs(Math.round(val))
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: { labels: { show: false } },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Transactions",
              data: [83, 153, 213, 279, 213, 153, 83]
            },
            {
              name: "Shipped",
              data: [-84, -156, -216, -282, -216, -156, -84]
            }
          ]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#revenueChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 300,
            type: "area",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            zoom: {
              enabled: false
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Revenue",
              data: [27000, 36000, 29540, 28500, 32000, 41000, 30000, 50000]
            }
          ],
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            curve: "straight",
            width: 2
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)"], // color var
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.9,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.1,
              stops: [0, 100, 100]
            }
          },
          xaxis: {
            type: "category",
            tickPlacement: "on",
            categories: ["", "January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            crosshairs: {
              stroke: {
                dashArray: 0
              },
              dropShadow: {
                show: false
              }
            },
            tooltip: {
              enabled: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontWeight: 400
              },
              formatter: title => (title ? title.slice(0, 3) : title)
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            stepSize: 10000,
            min: 0,
            labels: {
              align: "left",
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontWeight: 400
              },
              formatter: value => (value >= 1000 ? `$${value / 1000}k` : value)
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            y: {
              formatter: value => `$${value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}k` : value}`
            },
            custom: function (props) {
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props
              const title = categories[dataPointIndex]
              const newTitle = `${title}`

              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: newTitle,
                hasCategory: false,
                hasTextLabel: true,
                wrapperExtClasses: "min-w-30",
                labelDivider: ":",
                labelExtClasses: "ms-2"
              })
            }
          },
          responsive: [
            {
              breakpoint: 568,
              options: {
                chart: {
                  height: 200
                },
                labels: {
                  style: {
                    colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                    fontSize: "11px",
                    fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                    fontWeight: 400
                  },
                  offsetX: -2,
                  formatter: title => title.slice(0, 3)
                },
                yaxis: {
                  labels: {
                    align: "left",
                    minWidth: 0,
                    maxWidth: 140,
                    style: {
                      colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                      fontSize: "11px",
                      fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                      fontWeight: 400
                    },
                    formatter: value => (value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}k` : value)
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#revenueChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 300,
            type: "area",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            zoom: {
              enabled: false
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Revenue",
              data: [27000, 36000, 29540, 28500, 32000, 41000, 30000, 50000]
            }
          ],
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            curve: "straight",
            width: 2
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)"], // color var
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.9,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.1,
              stops: [0, 100, 100]
            }
          },
          xaxis: {
            type: "category",
            tickPlacement: "on",
            categories: ["", "January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            crosshairs: {
              stroke: {
                dashArray: 0
              },
              dropShadow: {
                show: false
              }
            },
            tooltip: {
              enabled: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontWeight: 400
              },
              formatter: title => (title ? title.slice(0, 3) : title)
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            stepSize: 10000,
            min: 0,
            labels: {
              align: "left",
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "14px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontWeight: 400
              },
              formatter: value => (value >= 1000 ? `$${value / 1000}k` : value)
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            y: {
              formatter: value => `$${value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}k` : value}`
            },
            custom: function (props) {
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props
              const title = categories[dataPointIndex]
              const newTitle = `${title}`

              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: newTitle,
                hasCategory: false,
                hasTextLabel: true,
                wrapperExtClasses: "min-w-30",
                labelDivider: ":",
                labelExtClasses: "ms-2"
              })
            }
          },
          responsive: [
            {
              breakpoint: 568,
              options: {
                chart: {
                  height: 200
                },
                labels: {
                  style: {
                    colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                    fontSize: "11px",
                    fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                    fontWeight: 400
                  },
                  offsetX: -2,
                  formatter: title => title.slice(0, 3)
                },
                yaxis: {
                  labels: {
                    align: "left",
                    minWidth: 0,
                    maxWidth: 140,
                    style: {
                      colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                      fontSize: "11px",
                      fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                      fontWeight: 400
                    },
                    formatter: value => (value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}k` : value)
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

      .apexcharts-flip-y {

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Onging Projects
        buildChart("#ongoingProjectsChart", () => ({
          series: [60, 20, 10],
          chart: {
            height: 275,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              hollow: {
                size: "50%"
              },
              track: {
                margin: 8,
                background: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
                strokeWidth: "10%"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  offsetY: 30,
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  fontWeight: 400
                },
                value: {
                  offsetY: -15,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: "24px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  formatter: function (val) {
                    return parseInt(val) + "%"
                  }
                },
                total: {
                  show: true,
                  fontWeight: 400,
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  label: "Total",
                  formatter: function (w) {
                    return "90%"
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-info)", "var(--color-accent)"],
          labels: ["Design", "Development", "Testing & QA"]
        }))

        // Upcoming Project

        buildChart("#upcomingProjectsChart", () => ({
          series: [25, 16, 8],
          chart: {
            height: 275,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              hollow: {
                size: "50%"
              },
              track: {
                margin: 8,
                background: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
                strokeWidth: "10%"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  offsetY: 30,
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  fontWeight: 400
                },
                value: {
                  offsetY: -15,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: "24px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  formatter: function (val) {
                    return parseInt(val) + "%"
                  }
                },
                total: {
                  show: true,
                  fontWeight: 400,
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  label: "Total",
                  formatter: function (w) {
                    return "49%"
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-info)", "var(--color-accent)"],
          labels: ["Design", "Development", "Testing & QA"]
        }))

        // Completed Project

        buildChart("#completedProjectsChart", () => ({
          series: [100, 100, 100],
          chart: {
            height: 275,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              hollow: {
                size: "50%"
              },
              track: {
                margin: 8,
                background: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
                strokeWidth: "10%"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  offsetY: 30,
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  fontWeight: 400
                },
                value: {
                  offsetY: -15,
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  fontSize: "24px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  formatter: function (val) {
                    return parseInt(val) + "%"
                  }
                },
                total: {
                  show: true,
                  fontWeight: 400,
                  fontSize: "14px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  label: "Total",
                  formatter: function (w) {
                    return "100%"
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-info)", "var(--color-accent)"],
          labels: ["Design", "Development", "Testing & QA"]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // External Link Chart
        buildChart("#externalLinkChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            type: "bar",
            height: 250,
            stacked: true,
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Google Analytics ",
              data: [75, 30, 76, 35, 40, 86, 35]
            },
            {
              name: "Facebook Ads",
              data: [25, 89, 25, 60, 60, 25, 60]
            }
          ],
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              horizontal: false,
              columnWidth: "35%",
              borderRadius: 6,
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadiusApplication: "around"
            }
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            curve: "smooth",
            width: 6,
            lineCap: "round",
            colors: ["transparent"]
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-base-content)"],
          fill: {
            opacity: 1
          },
          grid: {
            show: true,
            strokeDashArray: 10,
            borderColor: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)"
          },
          xaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          responsive: [
            {
              breakpoint: 467,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    columnWidth: "43%"
                  }
                }
              }
            },
            {
              breakpoint: 414,
              options: {
                plotOptions: {
                  bar: {
                    borderRadius: 4
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ],
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#reportBarChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 200,
            type: "bar",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              barHeight: "60%",
              columnWidth: "60%",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 4,
              distributed: true
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -20,
              bottom: 0,
              left: -10,
              right: -10
            }
          },
          colors: [
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)",
            "var(--color-primary)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, transparent)"
          ],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Earning",
              data: [150, 400, 300, 200, 380, 250, 320]
            }
          ],
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Mo", "Tu", "We", "Th", "Fr", "Sa", "Su"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "12px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            custom: function (props) {
              // Correctly accessing categories from xaxis
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props

              // Call the buildTooltip function and pass in the relevant options
              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: categories[dataPointIndex],
                hasTextLabel: true,
                labelDivider: ":",
                seriesExtClasses: "gap-2"
              })
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Performance Chart
        buildChart("#performance-chart", () => ({
          series: [60, 70, 80],
          chart: {
            height: 230,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          grid: {
            padding: {
              top: -10,
              left: -20,
              right: -20,
              bottom: -20
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            enabled: true,

            custom: function ({ series, seriesIndex, dataPointIndex, w }) {
              // Custom content for the tooltip
              const sales = series[seriesIndex]
              const displaySales = sales / 10
              const totalSessions = 10 // This is the maximum you want, could be dynamic
              const percentage = (sales / totalSessions) * 10
              const { globals } = w
              const { colors } = globals

              return `<div class="sb0ik mxjss px-2 qbqme">
    <div class="flex items-center rsqkx">
      <div class="kn3q0 ue1bl keg19" style="background-color: ${colors[seriesIndex]};"></div>
      <div class="text-xs font-medium">${globals.labels[seriesIndex]}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="flex justify-between epav6">
      <div class="text-xs font-medium">${displaySales}/${totalSessions}</div>
      <div class="text-xs font-medium">${percentage.toFixed(0)}%</div>
    </div>
  </div>`
            }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              hollow: {
                size: "20%"
              },
              track: {
                margin: 8,
                background: "var(--color-base-200)"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                show: false
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-warning)", "var(--color-error)"],
          labels: ["Golf Sessions", "Tennis Matches", "Gym Sessions"]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#sale-report-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 275,
            type: "bar",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          fill: {
            opacity: 1
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              horizontal: true,
              barHeight: "85%",
              distributed: true,
              startingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadiusApplication: "end",
              borderRadius: 6,
              dataLabels: {
                position: "bottom"
              }
            }
          },
          grid: {
            strokeDashArray: 10,
            borderColor: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 20%, transparent)",
            xaxis: {
              lines: {
                show: true
              }
            },
            yaxis: {
              lines: {
                show: false
              }
            },
            padding: {
              top: -35,
              bottom: -12
            }
          },
          colors: [
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-success) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-info) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-secondary) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-error) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-accent) 20%, #fff)"
          ],
          fill: {
            opacity: [1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1]
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: true,
            offsetX: 25,
            style: {
              colors: [
                "var(--color-primary)",
                "var(--color-success)",
                "var(--color-info)",
                "var(--color-secondary)",
                " var(--color-error)",
                "var(--color-accent)"
              ],
              fontWeight: 400,
              fontSize: "14px",
              fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
            },
            formatter: function (val, opt) {
              return val
            },
            dropShadow: {
              enabled: false
            }
          },
          labels: ["18520", "16520", "13900", "10200", "7600", "3650"],
          series: [
            {
              name: "Total",
              data: [18520, 16520, 13900, 10200, 7600, 3650]
            }
          ],

          xaxis: {
            categories: ["US", "IN", "CA", "JA", "AU", "NZ"],
            stepSize: 4000,
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 30%, transparent)",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontSize: "14px"
              },
              formatter: value => (value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}K` : value)
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            max: 20000,
            tickAmount: 6,
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 30%, transparent)",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                fontSize: "14px"
              }
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            enabled: true,
            y: {
              formatter: value => `$${value >= 1000 ? `${value / 1000}K` : value}`
            },
            custom: function (props) {
              // Correctly accessing categories from xaxis
              const { categories } = props.ctx.opts.xaxis
              const { dataPointIndex } = props
              const title = categories[dataPointIndex]
              const newTitle = `${title}`

              // Call the buildTooltip function and pass in the relevant options
              return buildTooltip(props, {
                title: newTitle,
                hasTextLabel: true,
                labelDivider: ":",
                seriesExtClasses: "gap-2"
              })
            }
          },

          legend: {
            show: false
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/lodash/lodash.min.js"></script>
  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/dist/helper-apexcharts.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        buildChart("#expenseChart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: "100%",
            type: "bubble",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            zoom: {
              enabled: false
            }
          },
          series: [
            { name: "Grocery", data: [[4, 8, 70]] },
            { name: "Food & Drinks", data: [[10, 9.5, 50]] },
            { name: "Shopping", data: [[8.5, 4.5, 40]] },
            { name: "Other", data: [[12.5, 5.5, 10]] }
          ],
          dataLabels: {
            style: {
              fontSize: "20px",
              fontWeight: "500",
              colors: ["var(--color-primary)", "var(--color-success)", "var(--color-error)", "var(--color-warning)"]
            },
            formatter: value => (value ? `${value}%` : "")
          },
          fill: {
            opacity: 1
          },
          colors: [
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-success) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-error) 20%, #fff)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-warning) 20%, #fff)"
          ],
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bubble: {
              zScaling: false,
              minBubbleRadius: 35
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            min: 0,
            max: 15,
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            min: 0,
            max: 15,
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            enabled: false
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          responsive: [
            {
              breakpoint: 565,
              options: {
                height: "80%"
              }
            }
          ]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
